Active LP implementation details:
```
def _active_lp_direction(f, c, active, n, M, r_max=80, Smax=4.0, Gmax=200.0/n):
    if len(active)<4: return None
    A = np.empty((len(active)+1, n))
    b = np.empty(len(active)+1)
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       A[idx,lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1]
       b[idx]=M
    A[-1]=1.0
    b[-1]=Smax
    c_obj = -np.ones(n)
    try:
       res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,Gmax), method='highs')
       if res.status in (0,3?) and res.x finite:
           g=res.x
           g=np.nan_to_num(g)
           s=g.sum()
           if s>1e-6: return g/s
    except: pass
    return None
```

Algorithm:
```
f=normalize(f); c=copy; L=2n-1; best...
deadline=...
D = min(16, max(6, n//400))? for n=6121 D=12; for small n D=8
R = D
max_rounds no improve 8
while time < deadline and no_improve<10:
   maxc=c.max()
   active = topK(c) with K depends (maybe 128)
   p = (c[active])**3 normalized? Or softmax temp small. To choose donors/receivers under active peaks.
   g = weighted_gradient(p,f)
   g_mean = f@g
   h = g - g_mean
   donor_order = sorted(-h) (move from high h to low)
   choose donors top D with f>eps; receivers bottom R not in donors.
   precompute units: for donor i: u_i zeros, u_i[i:i+n]= -f? Wait linear delta unit for moving dv from i to j: L_vec = e_j unit - e_i unit, where unit_i has f at i:i+n. In previous donor_units u_i=-f shifted at i; recv u_j=+f shifted at j. For pair, L = u_i + u_j. Quadratic as before.
   candidate fractions = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,0.999]
   best_move=None
   for i in donors:
      ui=unit +/-
      fi=f[i]
      for j in recv:
         unit_vec = ui+uj
         for frac:
            dv=fi*frac
            if dv<=1e-14 continue
            compute max via `np.maximum`? delta = 2*dv*unit_vec + quad. We can avoid allocating huge delta each time? We'll allocate delta once and fill.
   Apply best if improves.
   If no improve, no_improve++; optionally random pairs 50.
   Periodically renormalize/FFT every 20 moves.
```
But building unit vectors for each pair inside can be memory. Precompute `pos_unit` and `neg_unit` arrays? For each index, a length L vector with f shifted? n*L memory impossible. Precompute donor/recv units on the fly for D+R ~30; each length L 12k, memory 30*12k*8=2.9MB okay. We can store arrays.

```
lin = delta * (2*dv)
tmp = c + lin
tmp[2i]+=dv*dv
tmp[2j]+=dv*dv
tmp[i+j]-=2*dv*dv
mc=float(tmp.max())
```

def _initial_seeds(f0, n, rng):

 add uniform
 # Cow bits: xs linspace, mid
 for eps in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
    for xgrid in [lin,mid]:
       add clip(1+eps*cos(pi*x),0,inf)
       add clip(1-eps*cos(pi*x),0,inf) # reverse
 for alpha in [0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
    add exp(alpha*cos(pi*x)) and exp(-alpha*cos)

 for trial in 12:
    coeffs = rng.normal(0,0.3,K) ensure f=1+sum coeff*cos(k*pi*x + phase?) phases maybe 0 or pi? If phase random, not anti. Use cos(k*pi*x) with random sign. Maybe baseline 1.
    f=clip(1+sum,0,1)+1e-6; add
 # random smooth low freq with np.random and exp
 for trial 8: f=exp(0.5*sum random cos(2πmx+phase) + random linear); add
 # beta/skewed
 for trial 6: add (x+e)^a (1-x+e)^b
 # downsample/up sample from base

Implementation of `_local_search` more concretely:
```
def _local_search(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n <2 or budget_s<=0: c=_conv(f); return f, 2n*max
    c=_conv(f)
    maxc=max(c); best=2n*maxc; best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    deadline=now+budget_s
    it=0; no_improve=0; last_lp=-1e9; last_coord=now; last_perturb=now
    D_coord = ...
    while now < deadline:
        it +=1
        maxc=float(c.max()); cur=2n*maxc
        if cur < best: update
        remaining = deadline-now
        if remaining < 5: break
        improved=False
        # LP direction
        lp_interval = 6.0 if n < 4000 else (10.0 if n<10000 else 20.0)
        if now - last_lp > lp_interval and remaining > 10:
            if n <= 15000:
                active = _top_lags(c, max(32, min(128, n//32)), include_center=True)
                g = _active_lp_direction(f, c, active)
                if g is not None:
                    best_sc, best_ft, best_ct = _line_search_mixture(f,c,g)
                    if best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
                        f=best_ft; c=best_ct; improved=True
            last_lp=now
        if improved:
            no_improve=0; continue
        # Gradient steps
        # choose temps: if no_improve small use [0.005,0.02,0.08], else [0.001,0.005,0.02,0.08]
        for temp in temps:
            p = _softmax_weights(c, maxc, temp)
            h = _gradient_from_weights(p, f) - np.dot(f, h)
            scale = ...
            if scale <= 1e-14: continue
            h /= scale
            # multiplicative
            for eta in (0.2,0.5,1,2,4,8):
                arg=-eta*h; clip; ft=f*np.exp(arg); ft=_normalize(ft); ct=_conv(ft); sc=...; if best
            # perhaps euclidean for eta=1,4
            if best improved: apply; no_improve=0; break
        if improved: continue
        # coordinate polish short
        coord_budget = min(2.0, remaining*0.05) maybe; or if no_improve>5: min(10, remaining*0.1)
        if now - last_coord > coord_interval:
             fp,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(f,c,coord_budget,rng)
             if sc < cur: apply; no_improve=0
             last_coord=now
        if not improved:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve > 40 or (now-last_perturb>60 and no_improve>20):
                 f = _perturb(best_f, rng, strength=...)
                 c=_conv(f); no_improve=0; last_perturb=now
        # periodic print
        if it%200==0: print
    final polish? The coordinate inside budget.
    return normalize(best_f), best_score
```

Now, implement FFT convolution robust:
```
def _fft_size(m):
    if m <=2: return 2
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def _convolution(f):
    n=f.size
    if n==0: return empty
    if n==1: return np.array([f[0]*f[0]])
    m=2*n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)
    c=c[:m]
    # small negative due to roundoff
    # c[c<0 & c > -eps]=0? For max, negative irrelevant. But for LP M maybe max positive.
    return c
```
No need clip all.

`_fast_score(f)`:
```
f=_normalize(f); c=_convolution(f); n=f.size; if n==0 return inf; return 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
```

`_normalize` must handle zero sum. Use np.maximum and sum. If sum small, uniform.

`_interp_to_length`: linear. If n <2 handle. Ensure nonnegative. For small lengths, maybe use area-preserving? Linear okay.

`_smooth_with_window`: use np.convolve same; if win odd/even. Normalized.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    total_deadline=now + max(60,budget_s-35)
    f0 = load global or uniform 6121

```
def _coordinate_polish(f0,c0,budget_s,rng):
    f=normalize(f0); c=copy/conv; n=f.size; L=2n-1
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2n*max; deadline=...
    D = 12 if n<8000 else 8
    R = D
    fracs = [0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5,0.999]
    no_improve=0; moves=0
    while now<deadline and no_improve<10:
       maxc=c.max(); cur=2n*maxc
       if cur<best: update
       active,wt=_active_weights(c, K=... maybe 128)
       h=_gradient_direct(f,active,wt)*? - dot
       h_unit scale
       order=np.argsort(h) # low receiver, high donor
       donors: high h with f>eps top D
       receivers: low h not donors top R
       precompute pos_units? For each donor i: u=np.zeros(L); u[i:i+n]=f ... For receiver u with +f.
       best_move=None; best_mc=maxc
       for i in donors:
          fi=f[i]
          ui=zero; ui[i:i+n]=f (or -? pair unit = recv_unit - donor_unit; we can store donor negative? Let's store donor_unit with +f, and pair_lin = recv_unit - donor_unit)
       for j in recv similarly
       for i,j:
          lin = recv_unit[j] - donor_unit[i]
          for frac:
             dv=fi*frac
             total = c + 2*dv*lin
             q=dv*dv; total[2i]+=q; total[2j]+=q; total[i+j]-=2q
             mc=max(total)
             if mc<best_mc: best_move...
       # random pairs if no improve
       apply if best_mc<maxc - 1e-14
       if apply: f[i]-=dv; f[j]+=dv; c += delta_saved; moves++; no_improve=0
           if moves%20==0: f=normalize(f); c=conv(f)
       else: no_improve++; if no_improve>3: random perturb? maybe break
    final recompute
    return best_f,best_score,best_c
```

Let's consider memory/time of `np.argpartition` on c each iteration length L. Fine. But for direct gradient with active weights, we need active indices and weights. Function:
```
def _active_set(c, K, include_center=True):
    L=c.size; n=(L+1)//2
    r=min(L,K)
    if r==L: idx=np.arange(L)
    else: idx=np.argpartition(c, L-r)[L-r:]
    if include_center and n-1 not in idx:
        # replace smallest if needed
        if len(idx)>=r:
            order=np.argsort(c[idx])
            if len(idx)<L and c[n-1] > c[idx[order[0]]]:
                idx[idx[order[0]]]=n-1
            else:
                idx=np.append(idx,n-1)
        else: idx=np.append(idx,n-1)
    # maybe sort by c descending
    idx=np.unique(idx)
    # keep up to r+1 smallest c removed
    if len(idx)>K+1:
       order=np.argsort(c[idx])
       idx=np.delete(idx, order[:len(idx)-(K+1)])
    return idx
```
Simpler: create `top = np.argpartition(c, L-r)[-r:]`; if center not in top, `top = np.append(top, n-1)`. Then unique. Length r+1. Good.

Weights:
```
def _active_weights_c(c, active, q=3.0):
    vals=c[active]
    vals=np.maximum(vals,0)
    mx=vals.max()
    if mx<=0: weights=1/len
    else: weights=(vals/mx)**q
    s=weights.sum()
    if s<=0: uniform
    else weights/=s
    return weights
```
Maybe q=2 or 4. We can vary: q = 1 + 8*min(1, no_improve/20). More focus on stuck.

Direct gradient function:
```
def _gradient_direct(f, active, weights):
    n=f.size
    out=np.zeros(n)
    # for each idx,w
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
        w=float(weights[idx])
        if w==0: continue
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        # slice reversed
        out[lo:hi+1] += w * f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
    out *= 2.0
    return out
```
This is O(K*n). In Python loop K~128; each slice vectorized. Good. For K=256,n=20k =>5M ops per gradient, okay.

Gradient step function maybe:
```
def _gradient_improve(f,c,best_score_candidate, active, weights, n, etas):
   h=_gradient_direct(f,active,weights)
   mu=float(np.dot(f,h))
   h=h-mu
   hmax=float(h.max()); hmin=float(h.min()); rng_h=hmax-hmin
   if rng_h <=1e-15: return None
   h=h/rng_h
   best_sc=inf; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
   for eta in etas:
       arg=-eta*h
       # clip to avoid under/over
       np.clip(arg,-30,30,out=arg)
       ft=f*np.exp(arg)
       ft=_normalize(ft)
       ct=_convolution(ft)
       mc=float(ct.max())
       sc=2.0*n*mc
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_ct=ct; best_ft=ft.copy()
   if best_ft is not None and best_sc < 1e-18: return best_ft,best_sc,best_ct
   return None
```

`_initial_seeds(f0,n,rng)`:
```
seeds=[]
_add_seed(seeds,_interp_to_length(f0,n), "given")
_add_seed(seeds,_interp_to_length(f0[::-1],n),"rev")
for win in [3,7,15,31,63,127, max(5,n//40), max(7,n//16)]: add smooth
for gamma in [0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]: basep=(f0+1e-9)**gamma; add
_add_seed uniform
# grids
x0=np.linspace(0,1,n)
x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
for x in (x0,x1):
  for eps in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
     add np.maximum(1+eps*np.cos(np.pi*x),0)
     add np.maximum(1-eps*np.cos(np.pi*x),0)
  for alpha in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
     add np.exp(np.clip(alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x),-20,20))
     add np.exp(np.clip(-alpha*np.cos(np.pi*x),-20,20))
# random odd fourier
max_terms=6
for t in range(8):
   coeff=rng.normal(0,0.4,max_terms)
   f=np.ones(n)
   for idx in range(max_terms):
      k=2*idx+1
      f += coeff[idx]*np.cos(np.pi*k*x0) + 0.3*coeff[idx]*np.cos(np.pi*k*x1) ? just one grid.
   add np.maximum(f,0)
# random low Fourier even smooth

Pseudo code for `_local_search`:
```
def _local_search(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    f=_normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    deadline=t0+max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    c=_convolution(f)
    maxc=float(c.max()) if c.size else np.inf
    best_score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    if best_score >= np.inf: return f, best_score
    print(f"[{tag}] start score={best_score:.6f} n={n}")
    it=0; no_improve=0; last_lp=t0; last_coord=t0; last_perturb=t0
    K_small = max(16, min(96, n//64))
    K_mid = max(32, min(224, n//28))
    K_big = max(64, min(512, n//14))
    etas = (0.05,0.15,0.4,1.2)
    small_etas=(0.02,0.05,0.15,0.4)
    while True:
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline: break
        remaining=deadline-now
        if c.size==0: break
        maxc=float(c.max()); cur_score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
        improved=False
        # LP
        if now - last_lp > (5.0 if n<8000 else 10.0) and remaining > 15 and n <= 12000 and _HAVE_SCIPY:
            active=_active_set(c, max(32, min(80, n//80)), True)
            g=_active_lp_direction(f,c,active)
            if g is not None:
                res=_mixture_line_search(f,g)
                if res[1] < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    f=res[0]; c=res[2]; improved=True
            last_lp=now
        if improved:
            no_improve=0; it+=1
            if it%100==0: print...
            continue
        # coordinate if due
        if now - last_coord > 20.0 or no_improve > 8:
            coord_budget = min(8.0, max(1.0, remaining*0.05))
            if coord_budget > 1.0:
                fp,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(f,c,coord_budget,rng)
                if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    f=fp; c=cp; no_improve=0
                last_coord=now
                if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    it+=1; continue
        # gradient
        # cycle K
        it +=1
        if no_improve % 4 == 0: K = K_big
        elif no_improve % 4 == 1: K=K_mid
        else: K=K_small
        active=_active_set(c,K,True)
        q = 1.0 + 4.0*min(1.0, no_improve/20.0)
        w=_active_weights_c(c, active, q)
        h=_gradient_direct(f,active,w)
        mu=float(np.dot(f,h))
        h=h-mu
        hmax=float(h.max()); hmin=float(h.min())
        hscale=hmax-hmin
        if hscale > 1e-15:
            h=h/hscale
            # eta list depending no_improve
            if no_improve < 10: eta_list=etas
            else: eta_list=small_etas + (0.8, 2.0)
            best_sc=np.inf; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
            for eta in eta_list:
                if remaining <= 5: break
                arg=-eta*h
                np.clip(arg,-35.0,35.0,out=arg)
                ft=f*np.exp(arg)
                ft=_normalize(ft)
                ct=_convolution(ft)
                mc=float(ct.max()); sc=float(2.0*n*mc)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc=sc; best_ft=ft.copy(); best_ct=ct
            if best_ft is not None and best_sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                f=best_ft; c=best_ct; no_improve=0; improved=True
        if not improved:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 40 or (no_improve > 20 and now-last_perturb > 60.0):
                f=_perturb(best_f, rng)
                c=_convolution(f)
                no_improve=0; last_perturb=now
        if it % 250 ==0:
            print(...)
    # final recompute
    f=_normalize(f); c=_convolution(f); sc=...
    if sc<best_score: best_f=f; best_score=sc
    else? keep best_f from best_c maybe recompute
    bf=_normalize(best_f); bc=_convolution(bf); bsc=...
    if bsc < best_score: best_score=bsc
    print end
    return bf,best_score
```

```
def _active_set(c, K, include_center):
    L=c.size
    n=(L+1)//2
    K=int(max(2, min(L, K)))
    if K >= L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, L-K)[L-K:]
    if include_center:
       center=n-1
       if center >=0 and center < L and center not in idx:
          if len(idx) < L:
             idx=np.append(idx, center)
    idx=np.unique(idx)
    # maybe if length > K+1 keep largest K
    if idx.size > K+1:
       order=np.argsort(c[idx])
       drop=order[:idx.size-(K+1)]
       idx=np.delete(idx, drop)
    return idx
```
`center not in idx` uses array truth? `center in idx` works for np array (returns bool? Actually `in` uses `any(idx==center)`, okay but O(r). r small).

def _perturb(best_f, rng):
    n=best_f.size
    r=float(rng.random())
    if r<0.5:
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8))
       f=best_f*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif r<0.8:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
       noise=rng.random(n); s=noise.sum(); if s>0: noise/=s
       f=(1-alpha)*best_f + alpha*noise
    else:
       win=max(3, n//128)
       sm=_smooth_with_window(best_f, win)
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
       f=sm*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
    return _normalize(f)
```

...

import numpy as np
import time
try:

```
def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    f=_normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    if n < 2:
       c=_convolution(f); return f, 2nmax, c
    try: c=np.asarray(c0,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except: c=_convolution(f)
    if c.size != 2*n-1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): c=_convolution(f)
    maxc=float(c.max())
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=float(2.0*n*maxc); best_c=c.copy()
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    delta=np.zeros(2*n-1); total=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    no_improve=0; moves=0
    if n > 10000: D=8; R=8; Kpol=96
    elif n > 4000: D=10; R=10; Kpol=128
    else: D=14; R=14; Kpol=160
    fracs=(0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2,0.5,0.999)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 8:
       maxc=float(c.max()); cur=2nmax
       if cur<best: update
       remaining=deadline-now
       if remaining < 1: break
       active=_active_set(c, Kpol, True)
       w=_active_weights_c(c, active, 3.0)
       h=_gradient_direct(f,active,w)
       mu=float(np.dot(f,h)); h=h-mu
       if h.max()-h.min() <= 1e-15: no_improve+=1; continue
       order=np.argsort(h)
       high=order[::-1]; low=order
       donors=[]
       for idx in high:
          idx=int(idx)
          if f[idx] > 1e-14:
             donors.append(idx)
             if len(donors)>=D: break
       if not donors: no_improve+=1; continue
       donor_set=set(donors)
       receivers=[]
       for idx in low:
          idx=int(idx)
          if idx not in donor_set:
             receivers.append(idx)
             if len(receivers)>=R: break
       if not receivers: no_improve+=1; continue
       # precompute units
       L=2*n-1
       donor_units=[]
       for i in donors:
          u=np.zeros(L); u[i:i+n]=f; donor_units.append((i,u))
       recv_units=[]
       for j in receivers:
          u=np.zeros(L); u[j:j+n]=f; recv_units.append((j,u))
       best_mc=maxc; best_move=None; best_delta=None
       for i,ui in donor_units:
          fi=float(f[i])
          if fi <=1e-14: continue
          for j,uj in recv_units:
             if i==j: continue
             lin = uj - ui  # creates new array
             for frac in fracs:
                dv=fi*frac
                if dv <= 1e-15: continue
                np.multiply(lin, 2.0*dv, out=delta)
                q=dv*dv
                idx2i=2*i; idx2j=2*j; idxij=i+j
                delta[idx2i]+=q
                delta[idx2j]+=q
                delta[idxij]-=2.0*q
                np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                mc=float(np.max(total))
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc=mc
                    best_move=(i,j,dv)
                    best_delta=delta.copy()
                    # optional refine
                    # if not boundary, test nearby
       # refine if found and frac not boundary
       if best_move is not None and best_metric < maxc - 1e-15:
           i,j,dv=best_move
           # test fractions around dv/fi
           base=fi
           for scale in (0.5,1.5,2.0,0.7,1.3):
              d2=dv*scale
              if d2 <= 0 or d2 >= base*0.999: continue
              # need lin for i,j; recompute? We can find units? maybe skip for time.
           apply...
```

```
def _coordinate_polish(f0,c0,budget_s,rng):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    f=_normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    L=2*n-1
    if n <2 or budget_s <=0:
       c=_convolution(f); sc=...; return f,sc,c
    try: c=... copy
    except: c=_convolution(f)
    if c.size != L ...
    maxc=float(c.max()); best_score=...
    best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    work=np.zeros(L)
    Kpol = ...
    D/R ...
    fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.15,0.4,0.99)
    no_improve=0; moves=0
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now>=deadline: break
       maxc=float(c.max()); cur=...
       if cur<best_score: best...
       remaining=deadline-now
       if remaining < 1.0: break
       active=_active_set(c,Kpol,True)
       w=_active_weights_c(c,active,3.0)
       h=_gradient_direct(f,active,w)
       mu=float(np.dot(f,h)); h=h-mu
       hscale=float(h.max())-float(h.min())
       if hscale <= 1e-15: no_improve +=1; continue
       order=np.argsort(h)
       donors=[]; receivers=[] ...
       if not donors/receivers: no_improve +=1; continue
       best_mc=maxc; best_move=None; best_delta=None; best_frac=0.0
       for i in donors:
          fi=float(f[i])
          for j in receivers:
             if i==j: continue
             for frac in fracs:
                dv=fi*frac
                if dv <= 1e-15: continue
                mc,total=_transfer_max(c,f,i,j,dv,work)
                if mc < best_mc:
                   best_mc=mc; best_move=(i,j,dv,frac); best_delta=work.copy()
       if best_move is not None:
           i,j,dv,frac=best_move
           fi=float(f[i])
           for sf in (0.5,0.7,1.3,1.6,0.2,0.35,0.85,3.0):
               frac2=frac*sf
               if frac2 <=1e-4 or frac2 >=0.999: continue
               dv2=fi*frac2
               mc2,total2=_transfer_max(c,f,i,j,dv2,work)
               if mc2 < best_mc:
                   best_mc=mc2; best_move=(i,j,dv2,frac2); best_delta=work.copy()
       if best_move is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-14:
           i,j,dv,frac=best_move
           f[i]-=dv; f[j]+=dv
           if best_delta is not None: c=c+best_delta
           moves+=1; no_improve=0
           cur2=... if moves%20==0 recompute; else maxc=best_mc maybe
       else:
           # random
           rand_improved=False
           for _ in range(18):
              i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
              if i==j or f[i] <= 1e-14: continue
              frac=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.6))
              dv=fi*frac
              mc,total=_transfer_max(c,f,i,j,dv,work)
              if mc < maxc - 1e-14:
                 f[i]-=dv; f[j]+=dv; c=c+work.copy(); moves+=1; rand_improved=True; break
           if rand_improved: no_improve=0 else no_improve+=1
       if moves % 25 == 0:
           f=_normalize(f); c=_convolution(f); maxc=float(c.max()); cur=... update
    # final
    f=_normalize(f); c=_convolution(f); sc=...
    if sc < best_score: best_f=f; best_score=sc; best_c=c
    bf=_normalize(best_f); bc=_convolution(bf); bsc=...
    return bf,bsc,bc
```

Let's code `_local_search` with careful variable initialization.

```
def _local_search(...):
    t0=...
    f=...
    c=...
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=...
    ...
    while True:
       now=...
       if now>=deadline: break
       maxc=float(c.max())
       cur_score=float(2.0*n*maxc)
       if cur_score < best_score:
          best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
       improved=False
       # LP
       if _HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 12000 and (now - last_lp) > lp_interval and (deadline - now) > 60.0:
          lpK=...
          active=_active_set(c,lpK,True)
          g=_active_lp_direction(f,c,active)
          if g is not None:
             res=_mixture_line_search(f,g)
             if res is not None and res[1] < cur_score - 1e-12:
                 f=res[0]; c=res[2]; improved=True
          last_lp=now
       if improved:
          no_improve=0; iterations+=1
          if iterations % 200==0: print
          continue
       # coordinate if due
       if (now - last_coord) > 20.0 or no_improve > 8:
           coord_budget = min(8.0, max(1.0, (deadline-now)*0.05))
           if coord_budget > 1.0:
              fp,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(f,c,coord_budget,rng)
              if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                 f=fp; c=cp; improved=True; no_improve=0
              last_coord=now
              if improved:
                 iterations+=1; continue
       # gradient
       iterations+=1
       if no_improve < 8: Ks = K_small
       elif no_improve < 16: Ks = K_mid
       else: Ks = K_big
       active=_active_set(c,Ks,True)
       q=1.0 + 4.0*min(1.0, no_improve/20.0)
       w=_active_weights_c(c,active,q)
       h=_gradient_direct(f,active,w)
       mu=float(np.dot(f,h)); h=h-mu
       hscale=float(h.max())-float(h.min())
       if hscale > 1e-15:
          h = h / hscale
          if no_improve < 12: eta_list = (0.05,0.15,0.4,1.2)
          else: eta_list = (0.02,0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0,2.5)
          best_sc=np.inf; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
          for eta in eta_list:
             if deadline - now <= 5.0: break
             arg = -eta*h
             np.clip(arg,-35.0,35.0,out=arg)
             ft = f * np.exp(arg)
             ft = _normalize(ft)
             ct = _convolution(ft)
             mc=float(ct.max()); sc=float(2.0*n*mc)
             if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_ft=ft; best_ct=ct
          if best_ft is not None and best_sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
             f=best_ft; c=best_ct; improved=True; no_improve=0
       if not improved:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 45 or (no_improve > 25 and (now - last_perturb) > 90.0):
             f=_perturb(best_f,rng)
             c=_convolution(f)
             no_improve=0; last_perturb=now
       if iterations % 250 ==0:
          maxc2=float(c.max()); print
    # final
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for sequences minimizing

    score(a) = 2 * n * max_k (a * a)_k / (sum a)^2

where a is a non-negative coefficient sequence.

The search normalizes to sum(a) = 1 and uses:

  - FFT-based evaluation of full linear convolution a*a,
  - random / analytical / lifted low-resolution seeds,
  - coarse-to-fine optimization,
  - periodic active-set linear-programming directions (maximize sum(g)
    subject to fixed-f cross-convolution constraints and a sum bound),
  - multiplicative projected-gradient steps using weighted top-lag
    subgradients,
  - exact two-coordinate mass-transfer polishing,
  - random perturbations to escape local minima.

The final candidate is re-evaluated with evaluate_sequence when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
MAX_FINAL_N = 8000


def _normalize(v):
    """Project to a non-negative finite float vector of sum 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v


def _fft_size(m):
    """Smallest power-of-two FFT size at least max(2, m)."""
    if m <= 2:
        return 2
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _convolution(f):
    """Full linear convolution f*f via FFT. f is assumed normalized."""
    n = int(f.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(f[0]) * float(f[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)
    return c[:m]


def _fast_score(f):
    """Fast internal score for a non-negative sequence."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _convolution(f)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _interp_to_length(f, n):
    """Linearly resample f to length n and normalize."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = int(n)
    m = int(f.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return f
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, f))


def _smooth_with_window(f, win):
    """Uniform moving-average smoothing followed by normalization."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return f
    win = int(win)
    if win < 3:
        win = 3
    if win > n:
        win = max(3, n // 2)
    kernel = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    out = np.convolve(f, kernel, mode="same")
    return _normalize(out)


def _active_set(c, K, include_center):
    """Return active top lags, optionally adding the central lag."""
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    n = (L + 1) // 2
    K = int(max(2, min(L, K)))
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, L - K)[L - K:].astype(np.int64)
    if include_center:
        center = n - 1
        if 0 <= center < L and center not in idx:
            idx = np.append(idx.astype(np.int64), int(center))
    idx = np.unique(idx)
    if idx.size > K + 1:
        order = np.argsort(c[idx])
        drop = order[:idx.size - (K + 1)]
        idx = np.delete(idx, drop)
    return idx


def _active_weights_c(c, active, q):
    """Positive weights on active lags, emphasizing larger c-values."""
    if active.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.maximum(c[active], 0.0)
    mx = float(vals.max())
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.full(active.size, 1.0 / float(active.size), dtype=np.float64)
    w = (vals / mx) ** float(q)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(active.size, 1.0 / float(active.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def _gradient_direct(f, active, weights):
    """
    Gradient of weighted sum of convolution coefficients:
        h_i = 2 * sum_k weights[k] * f[k-i].
    Uses direct convolution for sparse active weights.
    """
    n = int(f.size)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        w = float(weights[idx])
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            out[lo:hi + 1] += w * f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
    out *= 2.0
    return out


def _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, work):
    """
    For moving mass dv from coordinate i to coordinate j, update the
    convolution coefficients by the exact increment in `work` and return
    the resulting max coefficient.
    """
    L = int(c.size)
    n = int(f.size)
    i = int(i)
    j = int(j)
    if i < 0 or i >= n or j < 0 or j >= n or i == j:
        return float(np.max(c))
    if dv <= 0.0:
        return float(np.max(c))

    work.fill(0.0)
    work[i:i + n] = -f
    work[j:j + n] += f
    work *= (2.0 * dv)

    q = dv * dv
    work[2 * i] += q
    work[2 * j] += q
    work[i + j] -= 2.0 * q

    total = c + work
    return float(np.max(total))


def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng):
    """
    Local search by exact two-coordinate mass transfers.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = int(f.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _convolution(f)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))) if c.size else np.inf
        return f, sc, c

    try:
        c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        c = _convolution(f)
    if c.size != L or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = _convolution(f)

    now = time.monotonic()
    deadline = now + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()

    work = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 0.99)
    refine_scales = (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.25, 1.6, 2.2)

    if n > 10000:
        D = 8
        R = 8
        Kpol = 96
    elif n > 4000:
        D = 10
        R = 10
        Kpol = 128
    else:
        D = 14
        R = 14
        Kpol = 160

    no_improve = 0
    moves = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now
        if remaining < 1.0:
            break

        maxc = float(c.max())
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

        active = _active_set(c, Kpol, True)
        w = _active_weights_c(c, active, 3.0)
        h = _gradient_direct(f, active, w)
        mu = float(np.dot(f, h))
        h = h - mu
        hscale = float(h.max()) - float(h.min())
        if hscale <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                break
            continue

        order = np.argsort(h)
        donors = []
        for idx in order[::-1]:
            idx = int(idx)
            if f[idx] > 1e-14:
                donors.append(idx)
                if len(donors) >= D:
                    break
        if not donors:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                break
            continue

        donor_set = set(donors)
        receivers = []
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in donor_set:
                receivers.append(idx)
                if len(receivers) >= R:
                    break
        if not receivers:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                break
            continue

        best_mc = maxc
        best_move = None
        best_delta = None
        best_frac = 0.0

        for i in donors:
            fi = float(f[i])
            if fi <= 1e-14:
                continue
            for j in receivers:
                if i == j:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    dv = fi * frac
                    if dv <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    mc = _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, work)
                    if mc < best_mc:
                        best_mc = mc
                        best_move = (i, j, dv, frac)
                        best_frac = frac
                        best_delta = work.copy()

        if best_move is not None:
            i, j, dv, base_frac = best_move
            fi = float(f[i])
            for sf in refine_scales:
                frac2 = base_frac * sf
                if frac2 <= 1e-4 or frac2 >= 0.999:
                    continue
                dv2 = fi * frac2
                if dv2 <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                mc2 = _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv2, work)
                if mc2 < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc2
                    best_move = (i, j, dv2, frac2)
                    best_frac = frac2
                    best_delta = work.copy()

        improved = False
        if best_move is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-14:
            i, j, dv, frac = best_move
            if best_delta is None:
                _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, work)
                best_delta = work.copy()
            f[i] -= dv
            f[j] += dv
            c = c + best_delta
            moves += 1
            no_improve = 0
            improved = True
        else:
            for _ in range(20):
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i == j or f[i] <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.65))
                dv = float(f[i]) * frac
                mc = _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, work)
                if mc < maxc - 1e-14:
                    delta = work.copy()
                    f[i] -= dv
                    f[j] += dv
                    c = c + delta
                    moves += 1
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 6:
                    break

        if moves % 25 == 0:
            f = _normalize(f)
            c = _convolution(f)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()

    f = _normalize(f)
    c = _convolution(f)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max())) if c.size else np.inf
    if sc < best_score:
        best_f = f.copy()
        best_score = sc
        best_c = c.copy()

    bf = _normalize(best_f)
    bc = _convolution(bf)
    bsc = float(2.0 * n * float(bc.max())) if bc.size else np.inf
    if bsc < best_score:
        best_score = bsc
    return bf, best_score, bc


def _mixture_line_search(f, g):
    """Line search along the mixture direction f -> g."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    g = _normalize(g)
    n = int(f.size)
    if g.size != n:
        return None
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_ft = None
    best_ct = None
    for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80):
        ft = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
        ft = _normalize(ft)
        ct = _convolution(ft)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * float(ct.max()))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_ft = ft.copy()
            best_ct = ct
    if best_ft is None:
        return None
    return best_ft, float(best_sc), best_ct


def _active_lp_direction(f, c, active):
    """
    Solve a bounded active-set LP:
        maximize sum(g)
        s.t.  (f*g)_k <= M  for active k
              sum(g) <= Smax
              0 <= g <= Gmax
    and return g normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = int(f.size)
    if active is None or active.size == 0 or n < 2:
        return None

    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    M *= (1.0 + 1e-12)

    # Keep LP matrix modest.  The central lag is included by the caller.
    max_vars_rows = max(16, min(96, n // 64))
    active = active[:max_vars_rows + 1]
    r = int(active.size)
    Smax = 3.0
    Gmax = max(0.02, 5.0 * float(f.max()))

    A = np.zeros((r + 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.empty(r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    b[:-1] = M
    b[-1] = Smax

    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            A[idx, lo:hi + 1] = f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

    A[-1, :] = 1.0
    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, Gmax), method='highs')
        if res is None or getattr(res, 'x', None) is None:
            return None
        if int(getattr(res, 'status', 1)) != 0:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
        np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1e-10:
            return None
        return g / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _perturb(best_f, rng):
    """Randomly perturb a good sequence to escape local minima."""
    f = _normalize(best_f)
    n = int(f.size)
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.5:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.85))
        f = f * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif r < 0.8:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.40))
        noise = rng.random(n)
        s = float(noise.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            noise /= s
        else:
            noise = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        f = (1.0 - alpha) * f + alpha * noise
    else:
        win = max(3, n // 160)
        sm = _smooth_with_window(f, win)
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
        f = sm * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
    return _normalize(f)


def _add_seed(seeds, f, tag=""):
    """Evaluate and append a candidate seed if finite."""
    try:
        f = _normalize(f)
        if f.size == 0:
            return
        sc = _fast_score(f)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            seeds.append((float(sc), len(seeds), str(tag), f))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _random_odd_fourier_seed(n, rng, terms=5, amp=0.35):
    """Random anti-symmetric odd-cosine seed on [0,1]."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx in range(terms):
        k = 2 * idx + 1
        f += float(rng.normal(0.0, amp)) * np.cos(math.pi * k * x)
    return np.maximum(f, 1e-9)


def _random_low_fourier_seed(n, rng, exponent=0.7):
    """Random low-frequency positive seed."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        u += float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45)) * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * x + phase)
    u += float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)) * (x - 0.5)
    return np.exp(float(exponent) * u)


def _random_beta_seed(n, rng):
    """Skewed beta-like positive seed."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
    b = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
    f = (x + 1e-4) ** a * (1.0 - x + 1e-4) ** b
    return np.maximum(f, 1e-9)


def _initial_seeds(f0, n, rng):
    """Build a diverse pool of starting sequences."""
    seeds = []
    base = _interp_to_length(f0, n)
    if base.size != n:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    _add_seed(seeds, base, "given")
    _add_seed(seeds, base[::-1], "reverse")

    for win in (3, 7, 15, 31, 63, 127, max(5, n // 40), max(7, n // 16)):
        _add_seed(seeds, _smooth_with_window(base, win), f"smooth{win}")

    for gamma in (0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        g = float(gamma)
        _add_seed(seeds, np.power(base + 1e-12, g), f"gamma{gamma}")

    _add_seed(seeds, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")

    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    for x in (x0, x1):
        for eps in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
            e = float(eps)
            _add_seed(seeds, np.maximum(1.0 + e * np.cos(math.pi * x), 0.0), "cos+eps")
            _add_seed(seeds, np.maximum(1.0 - e * np.cos(math.pi * x), 0.0), "cos-eps")
        for alpha in (0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00):
            a = float(alpha)
            _add_seed(seeds, np.exp(np.clip(a * np.cos(math.pi * x), -20.0, 20.0)), "expcos+")
            _add_seed(seeds, np.exp(np.clip(-a * np.cos(math.pi * x), -20.0, 20.0)), "expcos-")

    for rep in range(8):
        _add_seed(seeds, _random_odd_fourier_seed(n, rng, 5, 0.35), f"rOdd{rep}")
    for rep in range(8):
        _add_seed(seeds, _random_low_fourier_seed(n, rng, 0.7), f"rFour{rep}")
    for rep in range(6):
        _add_seed(seeds, _random_beta_seed(n, rng), f"beta{rep}")

    if n > 256:
        for K in (128, 256, 512, 1024):
            if K < n:
                coarse = _interp_to_length(base, K)
                _add_seed(seeds, _interp_to_length(coarse, n), f"lift{K}")

    if not seeds:
        _add_seed(seeds, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "fallback")

    seeds.sort()
    return seeds


def _coarse_seeds(f_best, K, rng):
    """Seed pool for low-resolution optimization."""
    seeds = []
    _add_seed(seeds, _interp_to_length(f_best, K), "global")
    _add_seed(seeds, np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")
    for rep in range(6):
        _add_seed(seeds, _random_odd_fourier_seed(K, rng, 4, 0.40), f"coarseOdd{rep}")
    for rep in range(4):
        _add_seed(seeds, _random_low_fourier_seed(K, rng, 0.8), f"coarseFour{rep}")
    if not seeds:
        _add_seed(seeds, np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64), "fallback")
    seeds.sort()
    return seeds


def _local_search(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Main local optimizer combining LP directions, gradient mirror steps,
    and exact coordinate polishing.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _convolution(f)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))) if c.size else np.inf
        return f, sc

    c = _convolution(f)
    maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else np.inf
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    K_small = max(16, min(96, n // 64))
    K_mid = max(32, min(224, n // 28))
    K_big = max(64, min(512, n // 14))

    eps = 1e-12
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_lp = t0
    last_coord = t0
    last_perturb = t0

    print(f"[{tag}] start n={n} score={best_score:.6f}")

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = deadline - now
        if c.size == 0:
            break

        maxc = float(c.max())
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

        improved = False

        # Active LP direction, if time and dimension allow.
        lp_interval = 6.0 if n < 8000 else 12.0
        if _HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 12000 and remaining > 60.0 and (now - last_lp) > lp_interval:
            lpK = max(24, min(96, n // 64))
            active = _active_set(c, lpK, True)
            g = _active_lp_direction(f, c, active)
            if g is not None:
                res = _mixture_line_search(f, g)
                if res is not None and res[1] < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    f = res[0]
                    c = res[2]
                    improved = True
            last_lp = now

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            it += 1
            if it % 500 == 0:
                print(f"[{tag}] iter={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={cur_score:.6f}")
            continue

        # Periodic exact coordinate polish.
        if (now - last_coord) > 20.0 or no_improve > 8:
            coord_budget = min(8.0, max(1.0, remaining * 0.05))
            if coord_budget > 1.0:
                fp, sc, cp = _coordinate_polish(f, c, coord_budget, rng)
                if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    f = fp
                    c = cp
                    improved = True
                    no_improve = 0
                last_coord = now
                if improved:
                    it += 1
                    continue

        # Weighted-gradient mirror step.
        it += 1
        if no_improve < 8:
            Kq = K_small
        elif no_improve < 16:
            Kq = K_mid
        else:
            Kq = K_big

        active = _active_set(c, Kq, True)
        q = 1.0 + 4.0 * min(1.0, float(no_improve) / 20.0)
        w = _active_weights_c(c, active, q)
        h = _gradient_direct(f, active, w)
        mu = float(np.dot(f, h))
        h = h - mu
        hscale = float(h.max()) - float(h.min())

        if hscale > 1e-15:
            h = h / hscale
            if no_improve < 12:
                eta_list = (0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 1.20)
            else:
                eta_list = (0.02, 0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 1.00, 2.50)

            best_sc = np.inf
            best_ft = None
            best_ct = None
            for eta in eta_list:
                if deadline - now <= 5.0:
                    break
                arg = h * (-float(eta))
                np.clip(arg, -35.0, 35.0, out=arg)
                ft = f * np.exp(arg)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _convolution(ft)
                sc = float(2.0 * n * float(ct.max()))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_ft = ft.copy()
                    best_ct = ct

            if best_ft is not None and best_sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                f = best_ft
                c = best_ct
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 45 or (no_improve > 25 and (now - last_perturb) > 90.0):
                f = _perturb(best_f, rng)
                c = _convolution(f)
                no_improve = 0
                last_perturb = now

        if it % 700 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] iter={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={cur_score:.6f} no_improve={no_improve}")

    f = _normalize(f)
    c = _convolution(f)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max())) if c.size else np.inf
    if sc < best_score:
        best_f = f.copy()
        best_score = sc
        best_c = c.copy()

    bf = _normalize(best_f)
    bc = _convolution(bf)
    bsc = float(2.0 * n * float(bc.max())) if bc.size else np.inf
    if bsc < best_score:
        best_score = bsc
        bf = _normalize(best_f)

    print(f"[{tag}] end best={best_score:.6f}")
    return bf, float(best_score)


def _add_candidate(candidates, score, f):
    """Keep up to eight internally-best candidates."""
    try:
        if not np.isfinite(score) or f is None:
            return
        item = (float(score), np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy())
        if len(candidates) < 8:
            candidates.append(item)
        else:
            worst = 0
            for i in range(1, len(candidates)):
                if candidates[i][0] > candidates[worst][0]:
                    worst = i
            if score < candidates[worst][0]:
                candidates[worst] = item
    except Exception:
        pass


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best non-negative coefficient sequence and return it
    as a Python list of floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_deadline = t_start + max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load the provided construction if available; otherwise fallback.
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1)
        if np.iscomplexobj(raw):
            raw = np.real(raw)
        f0 = raw.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    except Exception:
        f0 = None

    if f0 is None or f0.size < 3:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    f0 = np.nan_to_num(f0, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(f0, 0.0, 1000.0, out=f0)

    if f0.size > MAX_FINAL_N:
        f0 = _interp_to_length(f0, MAX_FINAL_N)

    n = int(f0.size)
    if n < 3:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)
        n = int(f0.size)

    best_f = _normalize(f0)
    best_c = _convolution(best_f)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(best_c.max())) if best_c.size else np.inf

    candidates = []
    _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)

    try:
        official0 = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_f])
        if np.isfinite(official0):
            print(f"Initial official score = {float(official0):.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"Loaded global length={n}, internal score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Initial seed pool.
    seed_pool = _initial_seeds(best_f, n, rng)
    if not seed_pool:
        seed_pool = [(best_score, 0, "given", best_f)]

    # Coarse-to-fine stage.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if n > 256 and (total_deadline - now) > 900.0:
        coarse_total = min(420.0, max(120.0, 0.18 * (total_deadline - now)))
        coarseKs = [256, 512]
        if (total_deadline - now) > 1500.0:
            coarseKs.append(1024)

        run_count = max(1, len(coarseKs) * 2)
        per_run = max(15.0, coarse_total / float(run_count))

        for K in coarseKs:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if (total_deadline - now) < per_run * 2 + 120.0:
                break
            seedsK = _coarse_seeds(best_f, K, rng)
            topK = seedsK[:2]
            for j, itemK in enumerate(topK):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if (total_deadline - now) < per_run + 90.0:
                    break
                alloc = min(per_run, total_deadline - now - 90.0)
                if alloc < 10.0:
                    break
                try:
                    cf, cs = _local_search(itemK[3], alloc, rng, f"coarse{K}_{j}")
                    if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_score:
                        best_score = float(cs)
                        best_f = _normalize(cf)
                        best_c = _convolution(best_f)
                        _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
                    lift = _interp_to_length(cf, n)
                    _add_seed(seed_pool, lift, f"coarse_lift_{K}_{j}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

        seed_pool.sort()

    # Main full-length local searches from best diverse seeds.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = total_deadline - now
    main_reserve = min(560.0, max(120.0, 0.22 * remaining))
    main_deadline = total_deadline - main_reserve
    main_budget = max(0.0, main_deadline - now)

    top = seed_pool[:4] if len(seed_pool) >= 4 else seed_pool
    weights = (0.52, 0.27, 0.14, 0.07)

    for idx, item in enumerate(top):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= main_deadline - 15.0:
            break
        remain_main = main_deadline - now - 10.0
        if remain_main < 30.0:
            break

        wsum = 0.0
        for wi in weights[idx:]:
            wsum += float(wi)
        if wsum <= 0.0:
            continue

        alloc = main_budget * float(weights[idx]) / wsum
        alloc = min(alloc, remain_main)
        alloc = max(20.0, alloc)
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = min(alloc, 0.62 * main_budget)

        if alloc < 15.0:
            break

        try:
            fopt, sc = _local_search(item[3], alloc, rng, f"main{idx}_{item[2]}")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_f = _normalize(fopt)
                best_c = _convolution(best_f)
                _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("main search exception:", repr(exc))

    # Final local search from current best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = total_deadline - now
    if remaining > 180.0:
        budget = min(280.0, remaining * 0.40)
        try:
            fopt, sc = _local_search(best_f, budget, rng, "final_local")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_f = _normalize(fopt)
                best_c = _convolution(best_f)
                _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final local exception:", repr(exc))

    # Final dedicated coordinate polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = total_deadline - now
    if remaining > 70.0:
        budget = min(300.0, remaining - 35.0)
        try:
            fpol, sc, cpol = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _convolution(best_f), budget, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_f = fpol
                best_c = cpol
                _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final coordinate exception:", repr(exc))

    # One last short local pass if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = total_deadline - now
    if remaining > 90.0:
        budget = min(120.0, remaining - 40.0)
        try:
            fopt, sc = _local_search(best_f, budget, rng, "late_local")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_f = _normalize(fopt)
                best_c = _convolution(best_f)
                _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("late local exception:", repr(exc))

    # Final exact coordinate polish with remaining safe time.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = total_deadline - now
    if remaining > 50.0:
        budget = min(180.0, remaining - 30.0)
        try:
            fpol, sc, cpol = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _convolution(best_f), budget, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_f = fpol
                best_c = cpol
                _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("last coordinate exception:", repr(exc))

    # Choose the best final candidate by official evaluation if time permits.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if len(candidates) > 1 and (total_deadline - now) > 45.0:
        best_official = np.inf
        best_official_f = best_f
        checked = 0
        for item in candidates:
            if checked >= 5:
                break
            sc_internal, cand = item
            if not np.isfinite(sc_internal):
                continue
            try:
                out = [float(x) for x in cand]
                off = evaluate_sequence(out)
                checked += 1
                if np.isfinite(off) and off < best_official:
                    best_official = float(off)
                    best_official_f = cand.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        if np.isfinite(best_official):
            best_f = _normalize(best_official_f)
            best_score = float(best_official)
            _add_candidate(candidates, best_score, best_f)
            print(f"Selected selected official score = {best_official:.6f}")

    # Final safety normalization.
    best_f = _normalize(best_f)
    best_f = np.nan_to_num(best_f, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(best_f, 0.0, 1000.0, out=best_f)
    s = float(best_f.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        best_f /= s
    else:
        best_f = np.ones(max(1, int(best_f.size)), dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_f]

    # Final official evaluation, ignored if it fails.
    try:
        final_official = evaluate_sequence(out)
        print(f"FINAL official score = {float(final_official):.6f}")
        print(f"Target score = {TARGET_SCORE}")
    except Exception:
        print("Final official evaluation unavailable.")

    return out
```
